rating: 5
review_text: This location is excellent for breakfast and brunch visits (they also offer lunch, but I’ve never eaten lunch here). I ordered a simple stack of pancakes, and they were nice. Other members of my party ordered French toast and the HamHash, which was the special for the day. Each thought the food was excellent, especially the French toast.

We did wait about 20mins for this visit on a late Sunday morning after church around 10:30. Every other time I’ve visited, there hasn’t been a wait to sit down. Customer service was excellent, as the head cook came out and greeted customers a time or two.

Overall, a great experience. I would highly recommend visiting sometime for a brunch with a few friends or family.

Visited in October of 2025

--- entity_reviews [row 1 of 5] ---
id: 46ac442a-aaa1-4661-8f9f-c7902dc1e9be
entity_slug: the-ugly-diner
reviewer_name: sizzlevegetables
rating: 5
body: This location is excellent for breakfast and brunch visits (they also offer lunch, but I’ve never eaten lunch here). I ordered a simple stack of pancakes, and they were nice. Other members of my party ordered French toast and the HamHash, which was the special for the day. Each thought the food was excellent, especially the French toast.

We did wait about 20mins for this visit on a late Sunday morning after church around 10:30. Every other time I’ve visited, there hasn’t been a wait to sit down. Customer service was excellent, as the head cook came out and greeted customers a time or two.

Overall, a great experience. I would highly recommend visiting sometime for a brunch with a few friends or family.

Visited in October of 2025
